The CoreServerMod implementation is complete and verified. Here's a summary:

## Implementation Summary

### ✅ Completed Features

1. **Sugar Cane on Soul Sand** - Placement and ~1 second growth via `SugarCaneBlockMixin` + `SugarCaneHandler`
2. **Accelerated Tree Growth** - Saplings grow in ~15 seconds via `SaplingGeneratorMixin` + `SaplingHandler`
3. **Iron Block Villager** - Villagers on iron blocks spawn golems every 5 seconds without vanilla requirements via `VillagerWakeMixin` + `IronGolemHandler`
4. **Accelerated Crop Growth** - Crops mature in ~15 seconds via `CropBlockMixin` + `CropGrowthHandler`
5. **Animal Breeding** - No cooldown, instant growth when fed via `AnimalEntityMixin` + `AnimalBreedingHandler` + `BabyGrowthHandler`
6. **Sheep Wool Regrowth** - ~5 second regrowth via `SheepEntityMixin`/`SheepShearedMixin` + `SheepWoolHandler`
7. **Villager Emerald Drops** - 40% chance on trade via `VillagerTradeMixin` + `VillagerEmeraldHandler`
8. **Guaranteed Chicken from Eggs** - 100% hatch rate via `ThrownEggEntityMixin` + `ChickenSpawnHandler`
9. **Chicken Egg Production** - Adults lay eggs every ~20 seconds via `ChickenEntityMixin` + `ChickenEggHandler`

### ✅ Technical Requirements Met

- **Minecraft 1.21.4**, Fabric Loader 0.16.9, Fabric API 0.118.0, Java 21
- Server-side only (`environment: "server"`)
- Configuration at `config/coreservermod.json` with validation/fallback
- Efficient tick scheduling (not per-tick for most features)
- Thread-safe operations on server thread
- Proper mixin targeting with intermediary mappings

### ✅ Build & Test

- **JAR**: `dist/CoreServerMod-1.0.0.jar` (47KB)
- **Test Server**: Starts successfully, mod loads, config loads, tick handlers register
- **Scripts**: `build.sh`, `start-test-server.sh`, `stop-test-server.sh`

### 📁 Project Structure
```
├── src/main/java/pro/codeservermod/
│   ├── CoreServerMod.java
│   ├── Config.java
│   ├── handler/ (9 handlers)
│   └── mixin/ (10 mixins)
├── src/main/resources/
│   ├── fabric.mod.json
│   └── coreservermod.mixins.json
├── build.gradle, settings.gradle, gradle.properties
├── build.sh, start-test-server.sh, stop-test-server.sh
├── test-server/ (complete server setup)
├── dist/CoreServerMod-1.0.0.jar
└── README.md
```
